We demonstrate predictive capability spanning the full spectrum of forest conditions ranging from sparse-canopy/low-biomass to dense-canopy/high-biomass. Results show that our model extends the range at which saturation is commonly encountered in comparable passive-sensor models, and reduces regression-to-mean behavior that commonly produces overestimation of forest attributes in small/sparse conditions and underestimation in large/dense conditions. The VibrantForests framework addresses a key limitation in large-area forest and wildfire planning by delivering coherent wall-to-wall estimates of management-relevant attributes at annual cadence and 10m resolution.

</details>


### [176] [Shifting-based Optimizable Linear Relaxations for General Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20292)
*Philipp Kern,László Antal,Erika Ábráham,Carsten Sinz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SLiR方法，一种广泛适用的神经网络激活函数线性松弛技术，仅需Lipschitz常数或关键点集，相比现有方法能验证多达7.8倍的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络验证方法依赖手工设计的激活函数线性松弛，扩展到新型激活函数需要大量人工工作，限制了验证方法的适用范围。

Method: 提出SLiR（基于平移的线性松弛）方法，通过斜率参数化松弛，使用平移过程计算偏移量，确保在输入域上获得可靠的上界和下界，仅需Lipschitz常数或关键点集即可实现高效优化。

Result: 实验表明，SLiR在多种实用激活函数上产生紧密的松弛，相比最先进方法能够验证多达7.8倍的性质。

Conclusion: SLiR提供了一种自动化、广泛适用的激活函数线性松弛方法，显著扩展了神经网络验证的适用范围和效率，减少了对手工设计的依赖。

Abstract: The use of neural networks (NNs) is rapidly increasing, including in safety- and security-critical domains. To provide formal guarantees about NN behavior, many verification methods rely on optimizable linear relaxations of activation functions. However, existing techniques depend on hand-crafted relaxations for each activation function. Extension to state-of-the-art activation functions therefore requires substantial manual effort. In contrast, our approach SLiR (Shifting-based Linear Relaxations) is broadly applicable, requiring only a Lipschitz constant or a set of critical points. SLiR parameterizes relaxations by their slope and computes the corresponding offset via a shifting procedure that ensures sound upper and lower bounds over the input domain, enabling efficient optimization while maintaining correctness. Our experiments show that SLiR produces tight relaxations across a wide range of practical activation functions and enables verification of up to 7.8x more properties compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [177] [Recurrent neural networks approximate continuous functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20325)
*Valentin Abadie,Clemens Hutter,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于[-1,1]上的任意连续函数，可以通过一个固定权重和固定隐藏层维度的ReLU循环神经网络，仅通过延长运行时间来实现任意精度的均匀逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典逼近定理在提高目标精度时需要构建新的神经网络。本文研究相反的可能性：能否一次性选定网络，仅通过延长运行时间来提升逼近精度？

Method: 提出了一种新的中间模型——神经单元图灵机（TMNU）。该模型既保留了实现多项式逼近方案所需的算法灵活性，又具有足够的刚性，可由具有显式隐藏维度和权重幅度界限的RNN模拟。

Result: 证明了每个[-1,1]上的连续函数都能被单个固定权重、固定隐藏维度的ReLU循环神经网络的时间演化所均匀逼近，收敛速率反映了底层的多项式逼近速率。同时给出了极小极大下界，证明运行时间在此固定网络逼近范式中是不可避免的资源。

Conclusion: 在网络固定的情况下，运行时间可以替代网络重构成为提升逼近精度的关键资源，这一发现挑战了传统逼近理论中需要重新构建网络的观点。

Abstract: Classical approximation theorems ask for a new neural network whenever the target accuracy is improved. This paper studies the opposite possibility: can the network be chosen once and for all, and can accuracy be bought only by letting it run longer? We prove that this is possible for every continuous function on [-1,1]. More precisely, each such function is uniformly approximated by the time evolution of a single ReLU recurrent neural network with fixed weights and fixed hidden dimension. The mechanism behind the construction is a new intermediate model, the Turing machine with neural units (TMNU). This model retains the algorithmic freedom needed to implement polynomial approximation schemes, while remaining rigid enough to be simulated by RNNs with explicit bounds on hidden dimension and weight magnitude. The resulting convergence rates reflect the underlying polynomial approximation rates. We complement the construction with minimax lower bounds showing that runtime is not merely a proof artifact, but an unavoidable resource in this fixed-network approximation paradigm.

</details>


### [178] [Quantum-classical physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold networks for PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20326)
*Xiang Rao,Yuxuan Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了QCPIKAN，这是首个用于求解偏微分方程的量子-经典物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络，通过结合切比雪夫多项式KAN层与参数化量子电路，在多孔介质渗流场景中展现出优于现有方法的预测精度和计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对偏微分方程求解的复杂性和计算挑战，现有量子-经典物理信息神经网络在预测精度和误差控制方面存在局限，需要开发一种能够加速高频误差收敛、有效缓解数值色散的高效求解框架。

Method: 构建基于切比雪夫多项式KAN层和参数化量子电路的混合框架，将物理约束嵌入训练损失函数中以强制物理一致性，利用近似理论进行理论分析和优化设计。

Result: 在多孔介质三种典型渗流场景（单相流、组分输运和两相流）验证中，QCPIKAN在全局预测精度、局部误差控制、动态演化追踪和驱替前缘定位方面均显著优于现有量子-经典物理信息神经网络，实现高频误差收敛加速至指数速率。

Conclusion: 该工作为求解复杂偏微分方程提供了一种高效、鲁棒的替代方案，证明了量子-经典混合架构在科学计算领域的应用潜力，为未来相关研究奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: We develop QCPIKAN, the first quantum-classical physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold network designed to solve partial differential equations (PDEs). Built upon Chebyshev-polynomial KAN layers and parameterized quantum circuits, this hybrid framework embeds physical constraints into the training loss to enforce physical consistency. Our theoretical investigations grounded in approximation theory prove that this design accelerates high-frequency error convergence to an exponential rate and effectively mitigates numerical dispersion. We validate the framework across three typical seepage scenarios in porous media, including single-phase flow, component transport and two-phase flow. Compared with existing quantum-classical physics-informed neural networks, QCPIKAN achieves superior performance in global prediction accuracy, local error control, dynamic evolution tracking and displacement front localization. This work provides a robust and efficient alternative for solving complex PDEs.

</details>


### [179] [Constrained hybrid modelling to predict microbial dynamics and organic matter turnover in soil systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20329)
*Paul Collart,Juergen Gall,Andrea Schnepf,Holger Pagel,Lars Doorenbos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了首个混合建模框架，能够基于宏基因组功能性状数据推导过程导向土壤有机质周转模型的生物动力学参数，在合成和真实数据上均优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土壤微生物控制有机质循环并对气候变化和环境威胁具有关键作用，但在过程导向的土壤模型中表征微生物动态非常困难。虽然整合基因组数据是改进参数化的有前途的方法，但如何建模基因组与其驱动的微生物过程之间复杂未知的关系仍是一个未解决的问题。

Method: 提出混合建模框架，利用神经网络从基因组性状数据预测过程模型的生物动力学参数，并整合生态学理论和文献约束以确保非观测状态变量的真实行为。该方法在具有不同复杂度的合成基因组性状数据集和真实数据上进行了评估。

Result: 该方法在多个基线方法上表现出性能提升，并且即使对于小规模训练数据集，也能有效学习过程模型中不可测量组分的动态特征。

Conclusion: 该混合建模框架成功解决了从DNA测序数据推导土壤有机质周转模型生物动力学参数的问题，为整合基因组数据改进过程导向土壤模型提供了有效的解决方案，在预测土壤碳循环方面具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Soil microorganisms control organic matter cycling and largely determine how soil systems can cope with and mitigate climate change and environmental threats. Representing microbial dynamics in process-based soil models is therefore critical to predict carbon cycling in soils, albeit highly challenging to inform from data. One promising approach to improve their parametrisation is the integration of genomic data, yet modelling the complex and unknown relationship between genomes and the processes the microbes are driving is an unsolved problem. In this work, we present the first hybrid modeling framework for deriving biokinetic parameter values of a process-based soil organic matter turnover model from metagenome-inferred functional traits based on DNA sequencing data. Our model predicts biokinetic parameters of the process-based model from genomic trait data with a neural network and integrates constraints from ecological theory and literature to ensure realistic behavior, even of non-observed state variables. We evaluate our method on synthetic genomic trait datasets of varying complexity and on real data, showing that our approach improves performance over multiple baselines and learns the dynamics of unmeasurable components of the process-based model effectively, even for small training datasets.

</details>


### [180] [Critical Percolation as a Synthetic Data Model for Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20347)
*Aryeh Brill,Tom Ingebretsen Carlson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于临界平均场渗流簇的合成数据集，用于评估可解释性方法。该数据集具有分层、多尺度结构和幂律统计特性，可通过线性时间算法高效生成。实验表明，神经网络激活中可以线性解码出数据的潜在层次变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的用于评估可解释性方法的合成数据集通常缺乏自然数据所具有的层次化、多尺度结构，限制了其作为现实玩具模型的价值。为了弥补这一差距，需要构建具有类似自然数据结构的合成数据集。

Method: 构建了一个在高维数据空间中嵌入临界平均场渗流簇的分层函数族作为合成数据集。利用渗流簇、随机树和可加凝聚之间的映射关系，提出了一个近线性时间算法，能够联合采样随机树及其分层潜在分解，支持任意规模的数据生成。该模型具有解析可处理性，临界指数固定其属性，无需超参数调整。

Result: 该数据模型具有稀疏性、自相似性、幂律统计特性和解析可处理性。通过探测实验发现，模型的ground-truth潜在变量可以从神经网络激活中线性解码出来。

Conclusion: 临界渗流数据集的稀疏性、自相似性、幂律统计特性和解析可处理性，使其成为可解释性研究的一个有原则的测试平台，为解决现有合成数据集的局限性提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Neural networks learn features that reflect the hierarchical, multi-scale structure of natural data. Synthetic datasets used to evaluate interpretability methods typically lack this structure, limiting their value as realistic toy models. To close this gap, we introduce a family of synthetic datasets consisting of hierarchical functions defined on critical mean-field percolation clusters embedded in a high-dimensional data space. The percolation data consists of sparse, low-dimensional fractal clusters with a power-law size distribution. Latent variables modeling a taxonomic hierarchy generate each data point's target value. The data model is analytically tractable with known critical exponents that fix its properties without requiring hyperparameter tuning. We leverage a mapping between percolation clusters, random trees, and additive coalescence to propose an almost linear-time algorithm to jointly sample a random tree and its hierarchical latent decomposition, enabling data generation at arbitrary scale. Using probing experiments, we find that the model's ground-truth latent variables can be linearly decoded from neural network activations. Together, sparsity, self-similarity, power-law statistics, and analytical tractability make critical percolation a principled testbed for interpretability research.

</details>


### [181] [On the Variance of Temporal Difference Learning and its Reduction Using Control Variates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20357)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了时序差分（TD）学习的方差性质，证明其方差渐近上有界于蒙特卡洛（MC）估计器，且较短视界的更新在固定样本数下产生更小的方差；同时将直接优势估计（DAE）解释为回归调整的控制变量，在极限情况下比TD具有更小的方差上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解时序差分学习为何能有效降低方差，并从理论角度量化其与蒙特卡洛估计器的方差关系，为设计低方差的强化学习算法提供理论依据。

Method: 采用相位设定下的表格表示法，分析TD学习的方差；通过证明TD有效聚合更多独立轨迹的机制，推导其方差上界；将DAE方法解释为回归调整的控制变量并进行理论分析；最后通过精心设计的环境进行数值验证。

Result: （1）TD的方差渐近上有界于MC估计器；（2）对于固定样本数，较短视界的更新产生更小方差；（3）DAE作为回归调整的控制变量，在大样本极限下比TD具有更紧的方差上界。

Conclusion: TD学习通过有效聚合多条独立轨迹来降低方差，DAE方法在此基础上通过回归调整进一步降低方差，为理解和改进强化学习中的方差降低技术提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We analyze the variance of temporal difference (TD) learning using the phased setting with tabular representation, and show that one of the mechanisms behind its ability to reduce variance is by effectively aggregating over a larger number of independent trajectories. Based on this insight, we demonstrate that (1) the variance of TD is asymptotically bounded from above by Monte Carlo (MC) estimators, and (2) shorter horizon updates incurs less variance for a fixed number of samples. Beyond TD, we show that Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE), a method for estimating the advantage function, can be seen as a type of regression-adjusted control variate, which achieves a tighter bound on the variance compared to TD in the large-sample limit. Finally, we numerically illustrate the behaviors of these estimators with carefully designed environments.

</details>


### [182] [Train, Retrieve, or Both? A Four-Arm Head-to-Head for Correct Statutory Citation on the Ontario Residential Tenancies Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20359)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究安大略省《住宅租赁法》法定引用任务中发现，SFT+RAG混合方法以0.481的精确匹配率表现最佳且零幻觉，优于单独的SFT和RAG，且无需专门的检索模型或更多训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自代理的租户、房东和帮助台工作人员需要被引导到实际管辖某个法律问题的法律条款，并给出正确的法定引用，这一需求推动了针对安大略省《住宅租赁法》（RTA）法定引用任务的研究。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上进行四组对比实验：基础零样本、仅LoRA SFT、仅RAG和SFT+RAG混合；在小型人工验证的真实评估集上以引用精确匹配（章节+小节）为指标进行评分。

Result: 基础模型无法引用RTA，仅SFT会错误回忆章节；检索是必要的，能按构造将幻觉降为零；SFT+RAG混合方法以0.481精确匹配率和零幻觉引用取得最高分，其优势来自SFT使条款选择对高召回候选集更稳健。该方法匹配或击败了基于更大专用检索模型的管道，且更大/改进的训练集无帮助：强法定引用性能不需要专用检索模型或更多数据。

Conclusion: 该成果消除了幻觉并超越了基线，但未达到期望的0.70精确匹配目标；所有结果基于小型待人工验证的真实评估集，作为初步结果报告。

Abstract: Self-represented tenants, landlords, and help-desk staff need to be pointed at the provision of law that actually governs a question, with a correct statutory citation. We study this task on the Ontario Residential Tenancies Act, 2006 (RTA) and its core regulation, asking the operator's question empirically: is fine-tuning enough, or is hybrid retrieval needed? We run a four-arm head-to-head on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (base zero-shot, LoRA SFT-only, RAG-only, and an SFT+RAG hybrid), scored on citation exact-match (section+subsection) over a small, human-verification-pending real eval set. The base model cannot cite the RTA and SFT-only mis-recalls sections; retrieval is essential and drives hallucination to zero by construction; and the SFT+RAG hybrid scores highest at 0.481 exact-match with zero hallucinated citations. Its edge comes from SFT making provision selection more robust to the higher-recall candidate sets that hurt zero-shot RAG. Notably, this cheap bge-small hybrid matches or beats a pipeline built on bigger, specialized retrieval models (a larger embedder and a cross-encoder reranker), and a larger/improved training set does not help either: strong statutory-citation performance here does not require specialized retrieval models or more data. The artifact zeroes hallucination and clears the lift-over-base bar but does not reach the aspirational 0.70 exact-match target. All results are on a small, human-verification-pending real eval set and are reported as preliminary.

</details>


### [183] [Judging to Improve: A De-biased VLM-as-3D-Judge Protocol for Single-Image 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20364)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究尝试利用VLM-3D评判模型来优化单图像到3D生成模型TRELLIS，尽管构建了优化级评判协议，但轻量级参数高效适应方法仅能达到与强基线持平的效果，未能超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否将已验证的VLM-3D评判模型的偏好用于专业化优化开放生成模型TRELLIS（针对家具类别），在无需人工标注的低成本条件下提升生成质量。关键挑战在于将评判模型从排序任务转变为优化训练信号。

Method: 提出优化级评判协议：1) 训练评判(Qwen2.5-VL-7B)与评估评判(InternVL3-8B)分离以打破循环性；2) 位置偏差校正；3) 修复三种失效模式（图像过载、几何隐藏的splat渲染、无参考评判偏好干净但错误的输出）；4) 质量对比构造工程化训练信号；5) 测试六种适配方法、两种输入机制和严重程度扫描。

Result: 校准证据显示清晰间隔胜率为0.83-1.0，基线对比约0.5；最目标化的方法（严重退化下的条件器修复）达到与基线持平(0.50)，但所有方法均未达到>=65%胜率目标。发现：干净输入使评判饱和，Flow-DIT微调通过采样器被冲淡，条件修复是改变几何的关键位置。

Conclusion: 使用轻量级PEFT在公共数据上仅能匹配强基线而无法超越，这本身具有信息价值——超越强基线需要比轻量级适配更多资源。评判协议可复用，为后续研究提供了可借鉴的实验框架。

Abstract: A companion study established a de-biased, cross-model VLM-as-3D-judge that reliably ranks single-image-to-3D mesh quality where cheap geometry and CLIP proxies fall short. This paper asks: can that judge's preferences specialize a strong open generator, TRELLIS, on one asset class (furniture), cheaply and without human labels? Taking the judge from ranking to optimization is where the work lives. Pushing a VLM judge into the training and evaluation loop exposes failure modes ranking never triggered, so our contribution is an optimization-grade hardening of the judge: a training judge (Qwen2.5-VL-7B) held distinct from an evaluation judge (InternVL3-8B) to break circularity; position-bias correction; and fixes for three failure modes (image overload, geometry-hiding splat renders, and reference-free judging that rewards clean-but-wrong outputs), with calibration evidence (clear-gap win-rate 0.83-1.0; base-vs-base ~0.5). Using this protocol as an independent evaluator, and working only from public models and data with lightweight parameter-efficient adaptation, we find our methods match the strong base rather than exceed it. Independent base samples carry essentially no learnable preference (0.94 order-flip rate), so signal must be engineered by quality-contrastive construction. Across six adaptation methods, two input regimes, and a severity sweep, the most targeted - conditioner repair under severe degradation - reaches parity (0.50) with the base, while no method clears the >=65% win-rate target. The result is mechanistic: clean inputs saturate the judge, flow-DIT fine-tuning washes out through the sampler, and conditioning repair is the locus that moves geometry. Win-rates are directional at n=8 objects. Matching a strong public-data base with cheap adaptation is itself informative: exceeding it needs more than lightweight PEFT on public data, and the judge protocol is reusable.

</details>


### [184] [CRAX: Fast Safe Reinforcement Learning Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20376)
*Tristan Tomilin,Mourad Boustani,Mickey Beurskens,Thiago D. Simão*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CRAX，一个基于JAX的高性能安全强化学习基准，相比CPU基准实现约100倍加速，包含6个环境套件和3个智能体任务，评估发现没有单一方法在所有任务中占优，课程学习和安全迁移能提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具有高保真3D物理仿真的安全强化学习基准计算速度慢，限制了大尺度实验和快速原型开发，需要高效的安全RL基准来促进研究进展。

Method: 基于MuJoCo XLA (MJX)物理引擎构建CRAX基准，利用向量化操作和硬件加速技术实现高保真3D动力学仿真，设计包含6个环境套件、3个智能体任务和3个难度等级的任务体系，评估6种流行的安全RL方法。

Result: 相比可比的CPU安全基准获得约100倍加速；评估显示没有单一方法在所有任务中占优，揭示了性能与安全性之间的权衡；发现跨难度等级的课程学习和安全迁移在更困难设置中比直接训练能改善性能。

Conclusion: CRAX成功解决了现有安全RL基准的计算效率瓶颈，通过大规模实验揭示了安全RL方法的特点和权衡，为安全强化学习研究提供了高效的实验平台。

Abstract: Safety is a core concern for deploying reinforcement learning (RL) agents in real-world domains such as robotics and autonomous driving. While benchmarks have been central to progress in RL, existing safety benchmarks with high-fidelity 3D physics remain computationally slow, limiting large-scale experimentation and rapid prototyping. To address this gap, we propose CRAX (Constrained RL Accelerated with JAX). Built on top of the MuJoCo XLA (MJX) physics engine with realistic 3D dynamics, CRAX leverages vectorized operations and hardware acceleration, yielding up to ~100x speedups over comparable CPU-based safety benchmarks. The benchmark features six environment suites and three agent-specific tasks, each spanning three difficulty levels. Evaluating six popular safe RL methods shows that no single approach dominates across all tasks, and reveals the trade-offs between performance and safety. We find that curriculum learning across difficulty levels and safety transfer can improve performance over direct training in harder settings.

</details>


### [185] [Towards Modality-imbalanced Federated Graph Learning: A Data Synthesis-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20382)
*Zhengyu Wu,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FedMGS框架，用于解决多模态联邦图学习中客户端级和节点级的模态缺失问题，在四个任务上性能提升高达17.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦图学习在实际部署中面临两种粒度的模态缺失挑战：客户端级缺失（某些客户端缺少完整模态）和节点级缺失（个别节点缺失视觉或文本属性）。现有研究主要针对图无关或中心化场景，难以直接适配，因此需要专门针对联邦图学习场景的解决方案。

Method: 将模态缺失的MM-FGL形式化为隐式图感知潜在语义表示合成问题，提出FedMGS框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）可用性感知图编码器，防止缺失模态污染局部结构传播；（2）原型引导的潜在语义合成器，为缺失模态建立跨客户端语义锚点；（3）可靠性校准的语义融合机制，在预测读出前调节恢复的潜在表示的影响。

Result: 在四个任务上的广泛实验表明，FedMGS在性能上持续优于竞争基线，性能增益高达17.41%，并在效率-性能权衡方面达到最佳水平。

Conclusion: FedMGS通过直接在表示空间中恢复缺失的模态语义，实现了与原始数据语义分布的最大对齐，有效缓解了缺失模态引起的高方差问题，为模态缺失的多模态联邦图学习提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: MultiModal Federated Graph Learning (MM-FGL) offers a natural collaborative training paradigm, but its practical deployment is challenged by two granularities of modality imbalance. Client-level imbalance occurs when certain clients lack entire modalities, while node-level imbalance occurs when individual nodes exhibit missing visual or textual attributes. While several relevant studies exist, our investigation reveals that they predominantly target graph-agnostic or centralized scenarios, rendering them difficult to adapt directly. To address these challenges, we formalize modality-imbalanced MM-FGL as an implicit graph-aware latent semantic representation synthesis problem. This paradigm recovers missing modal semantics directly within the representation space, thereby maximizing alignment with the original data's semantic distribution and mitigating the high variance induced by missing modalities. To this end, we propose FedMGS (Federated Modality-aware Graph Synthesis), which integrates three core components. The availability-aware graph encoder prevents missing modalities from contaminating local structural propagation. The prototype-guided latent semantic synthesizer establishes cross-client semantic anchors for unavailable modalities. The reliability-calibrated semantic fusion mechanism regulates the impact of recovered latent representations prior to predictive readout. Extensive experiments on four tasks show that FedMGS consistently outperforms competitive baselines with gains up to 17.41% with best efficiency-performance tradeoff.

</details>


### [186] [The Significance of Style Diversity in Annotation-Free Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20400)
*Zahra Abbasiantaeb,Zeno Belligoli,Omar Essam,Mohammad Aliannejadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无需人工标注数据的合成对话生成框架，仅依赖意图定义即可生成高质量训练数据，达到了人工标注数据93.3%的性能，并发现风格多样性比主题多样性更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在快速变化的工业环境中，生成用于意图分类的高效用合成数据通常需要人工标注的种子数据，但这些数据往往不可用或获取成本高昂，亟需一种不依赖人工标注的数据生成方法。

Method: 提出一个完全无需人工标注的合成对话生成框架，仅依赖意图定义；利用两类主题和风格属性提高数据多样性；设计了两种事后风格化模型（Univ和Exam）将LLM生成的语句转化为更自然多样的风格；采用LLM-as-a-judge过滤流程提升数据质量。

Result: 在工业和公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法可达到使用人工标注训练数据93.3%的性能；关键发现是风格多样性比主题多样性更能提升合成数据效用，可防止模型学习虚假的风格相关性；在生成过程中融入风格属性比事后风格适应更有效。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在无人工标注数据情况下生成高质量意图分类训练数据的可行性，为工业场景下的快速部署提供了实用解决方案；揭示了风格多样性在合成数据质量中的核心作用，为未来合成数据生成研究提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Generating high-utility synthetic data for intent classification typically requires human-annotated seed data, which is often unavailable in fast-paced industrial settings. In this paper, we propose a framework for synthetic dialogue generation that works entirely without human-annotated data, relying solely on intent definitions. Our proposed dialogue generation framework utilizes two different types of topic and style attributes to improve data diversity. Also, we propose two novel post-hoc stylization models called Univ and Exam to transform synthetic LLM-generated utterances into more varied, human-like linguistic styles. To enhance data quality, we utilize an LLM-as-a-judge filtering process. Experimental results on both industrial and public datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves up to 93.3% of the performance obtained using human-annotated training data. Crucially, the findings reveal that style diversity is more critical than topic diversity for synthetic data utility, as it prevents models from learning spurious stylistic correlations. Furthermore, the study shows that incorporating style attributes during the generation process is more effective than post-hoc style adaptation.

</details>


### [187] [Direct Advantage Estimation for Scalable and Sample-efficient Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20411)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了直接优势估计(DAE)方法至部分可观测环境，并通过离散潜在动力学模型降低计算开销，在Atari游戏中验证了方法的有效性和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DAE方法存在两个主要局限：1)需要完全可观测环境，限制了其在实际场景中的应用；2)需要建模转移概率，在高维观测空间下计算开销巨大。

Method: 1)将DAE理论框架扩展到部分可观测域，仅需最小改动；2)引入离散潜在动力学模型来高效近似转移概率，从而降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在Arcade Learning Environment上的实验表明，DAE能够随函数近似器容量有效扩展，同时保持高样本效率。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了DAE在实际应用中的两大限制，使其能够适用于部分可观测环境，并在高维观测空间下保持计算效率，为深度强化学习在实际复杂场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE) has been shown to improve the sample efficiency of deep reinforcement learning algorithms. However, its reliance on full environment observability limits its applicability in realistic settings, and its requirement to model transition probabilities incurs substantial computational overhead for high-dimensional observations. In the present work, we address both limitations. First, we extend the theoretical framework of DAE to partially observable domains with minimal modifications. Second, we reduce its computational complexity by introducing discrete latent dynamics models that efficiently approximate transition probabilities. We evaluate our approach on the Arcade Learning Environment and find that DAE scales effectively with function approximator capacity while retaining high sample efficiency.

</details>


### [188] [Pseudo-Feature Padding: A Lightweight Defense Against False Data Injection in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20415)
*Farhin Farhad Riya,Shahinul Hoque,Yingyuan Yang,Jinyuan Sun,Kevin Tomsovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、模型无关的防御框架，通过在输入层添加基于统计分布的伪特征填充来增强深度神经网络对虚假数据注入攻击的鲁棒性，在电力系统应用中显著提升了模型安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在信息物理系统（CPS）中用于检测虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA）时表现出色，但CPS的独特基础设施使DNN容易受到攻击者的利用以逃避检测。同时，CPS的独特性质也给传统的FDIA防御机制带来了挑战，亟需新的防御方案。

Method: 提出一种创新的防御框架，通过引入额外的输入层，利用从输入统计分布中导出的伪特征值对输入样本进行填充。这种填充以随机和数据感知的方式增加输入维度，使得对抗攻击由于精心设计的扰动不可转移以及填充结构的不可预测性而在计算上变得不可行。该方法轻量级、模型无关，无需修改核心架构。

Result: 在IEEE 14母线、30母线、118母线和300母线系统的关键电力网格应用（如状态估计）上进行评估。实验表明，该填充策略在对抗环境下显著提高了模型鲁棒性，对性能影响可忽略不计，并能有效缓解那些原本可以绕过传统防御的攻击。

Conclusion: 所提出的防御框架成功增强了DNN在CPS环境下对抗FDIA攻击的能力，具有轻量级、高可部署性的特点，为实际CPS环境提供了一种有效的防御解决方案。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks DNNs have achieved remarkable accuracy in various tasks including their application in CyberPhysical Systems CPS for detecting False Data Injection Attacks FDIA during critical operations However the unique infrastructure of CPS makes DNNs vulnerable to exploitation by attackers aiming to evade detection Additionally the distinct nature of CPS presents challenges for conventional defense mechanisms against FDIA This paper proposes an innovative defense framework that strengthens DNNs against such attacks by introducing an additional input layer that performs padding in the input samples using pseudofeature values derived from the inputs statistical distribution This padding increases the input dimensionality in a randomized and dataaware manner making adversarial attacks computationally infeasible due to the nontransferable nature of crafted perturbations and the unpredictability of the padded structure Our method is lightweight modelagnostic and requires no modifications to the core architecture making it highly deployable in realworld CPS settings We evaluated our framework on critical power grid applications such as state estimation using the IEEE 14bus 30bus 118bus and 300bus systems Experiments under adversarial settings demonstrate that our padding strategy significantly improves model robustness with negligible impact on performance and effectively mitigates attacks that would otherwise bypass conventional defenses

</details>


### [189] [UltraQuant: 4-bit KV Caching for Context-Heavy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20474)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,David Limpus,Aditi Ghai Rana,Bowen Bao,Spandan Tiwari,Thiago Crepaldi,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对长上下文智能体场景，提出了4-bit KV缓存压缩方案。在AMD GPU上实现的UltraQuant优化路径，将P50首令牌时间降低3.47倍，输出吞吐量提升1.63倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文密集型智能体对KV缓存施加了不寻常的压力：长前缀在多个短轮次中被重复使用，而并发性决定了服务系统是否能充分利用GPU。现有缓存方案在质量、缓存驻留和服务吞吐量之间存在权衡，需要针对智能体工作负载进行专门优化。

Method: 采用TurboQuant风格的旋转和码本量化作为质量基准，vLLM FP8 KV缓存作为部署基准。关键技术包括：非对称K/V处理、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、移除QJL、块尺度变体。在AMD GPU上实现了服务优化，包括优化的解码注意力内核和UltraQuant——一种FP4近似路径，使用FP8查询、FP4 KV张量、UE8M0组尺度和CDNA4原生scaled-MFMA支持。

Result: 在长上下文、多轮智能体工作负载测试中，UltraQuant在缓存压力较大的后轮次中将P50首令牌时间降低了3.47倍（所有轮次平均降低2.3倍），相比FP8 KV基线将输出吞吐量提升了1.63倍。

Conclusion: 4-bit KV缓存在智能体工作负载中是可行的，通过精心设计的量化策略和硬件优化，可以在保持任务质量的同时显著降低延迟并提升吞吐量。这为部署长上下文智能体应用提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Context-heavy agents place unusual pressure on the key-value (KV) cache: long prefixes are reused across many short turns, while concurrency determines whether the serving system can keep GPUs utilized. We study 4-bit KV-cache compression for this setting, using TurboQuant-style rotation and codebook quantization as a quality anchor and vLLM FP8 KV caching as the deployment anchor. We report three contributions. First, we frame 4-bit KV caching around multi-round agent workloads where task quality, cache residency, and serving throughput must be measured jointly. Second, we describe the practical design choices needed to make the 4-bit path robust, including asymmetric K/V treatment, Walsh-Hadamard rotation, QJL removal, and block-scale variants. Third, we present serving optimizations on AMD GPUs, including optimized decode-attention kernels and UltraQuant, an FP4 approximation path that uses FP8 queries, FP4 KV tensors, UE8M0 group scales, and native scaled-MFMA support on CDNA4. On a long-context, multi-turn agentic workload, UltraQuant cuts P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in the cache-pressured late rounds (2.3x across all rounds) and raises output throughput by 1.63x over the FP8 KV baseline.

</details>


### [190] [On the Redundancy of Timestep Embeddings in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20416)
*José A. Chávez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了扩散模型必须依赖显式时间步嵌入的传统范式，理论和实验证明在特定条件下可完全移除时间步条件，且模型性能不减反增。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型传统上依赖显式的时间步嵌入来调节去噪过程。本研究旨在挑战这一长期假设，质疑时间步信号的必要性，探索模型是否可以在没有显式时间条件的情况下工作。

Method: 分析时间步嵌入对U-Net和Diffusion Transformer架构的影响；建立理论框架证明在特定条件下，扩散训练目标的全局最小化器可以在没有显式时间步条件的情况下实现；在CelebA和CIFAR-10数据集上进行广泛的消融实验，比较有无时间步条件的模型性能。

Result: 完全移除时间步嵌入后，模型表现出惊人的鲁棒性；在FID、precision和recall等竞争性指标上，时间无关模型甚至能够超越有条件对应的模型；模型能够在特定假设下从损坏的输入中隐式推断噪声尺度。

Conclusion: 显式时间步条件在特定条件下是冗余的；架构可以从输入中隐式推断噪声尺度；该发现为构建更高效、更注重结构的生成架构铺平道路，挑战了长期存在的时间条件范式。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely heavily on explicit timestep embeddings to modulate the denoising process across various noise scales. In this work, we challenge the necessity of these temporal signals by analyzing their impact on U-Net and Diffusion Transformer architectures. Beyond empirical evidence, we provide a theoretical framework demonstrating that, under certain conditions, the global minimizer of the diffusion training objective can be achieved without explicit timestep conditioning. Our findings reveal a surprising robustness when timestep embeddings are completely removed. Extensive ablation studies on the CelebA and CIFAR-10 datasets show that these time-agnostic models can maintain high structural fidelity and even surpass their conditioned counterparts in competitive metrics, including FID, precision, and recall. Our analysis suggests these architectures can implicitly infer noise scales from the corrupted input under specific assumptions, rendering explicit temporal conditioning redundant. This study challenges long-standing temporal conditioning paradigms and paves the way for more efficient and structurally focused generative architectures.

</details>


### [191] [Neural network surrogates with uncertainty quantification for inverse problems in partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20417)
*Christian Jimenez-Beltran,Aretha L. Teckentrup,Antonio Vergari,Konstantinos C. Zygalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeepGaLA，一种用于微分方程求解器的神经网络代理模型，能够提供不确定性感知的预测，解决了传统数值方法在贝叶斯逆问题中计算成本高的问题，并通过延迟接受MCMC验证了其后验近似的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微分方程逆问题在科学和工程中广泛存在，传统数值方法在贝叶斯框架下计算昂贵，特别是在复杂前向模型和高维参数空间中评估似然函数的成本很高。现有方法难以同时满足计算效率和不确定性量化的需求。

Method: 提出DeepGaLA神经网络代理模型用于近似微分方程求解器，该模型具有不确定性感知能力，能够在训练数据有限时减少过度自信的推断。采用延迟接受马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）作为诊断工具，评估代理模型诱导的后验近似的保真度。该框架能够融合微分方程约束，包括非线性情形。

Result: 在多项数值实验中，DeepGaLA提供的前向模型近似精度与已建立的高斯过程代理模型相当，但随着参数维度的增长能更好地保持计算效率。延迟接受MCMC的短期运行可有效诊断代理模型的保真度。该方法成功融合了微分方程约束。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，具有不确定性量化的神经网络代理模型能够为复杂系统中的逆问题实现可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯推断，为解决高维参数空间中的计算挑战提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Inverse problems for differential equations arise throughout science and engineering, where one seeks to infer unknown model parameters from noisy or incomplete observations. Traditional numerical methods for these problems are often computationally expensive, particularly in Bayesian settings where evaluating the likelihood becomes costly for complex forward models and high-dimensional parameter spaces. To address this challenge, we introduce DeepGaLA, a neural-network surrogate for differential equation solvers that provides uncertainty-aware predictions, reducing overconfident inference when training data are limited. To evaluate the fidelity of the surrogate-induced posterior approximations in practice, we show that a short run of delayed-acceptance Markov chain Monte Carlo can serve as an effective diagnostic. Across a range of numerical experiments, DeepGaLA delivers forward-model approximations with accuracy comparable to established Gaussian-process surrogates, while better maintaining efficiency as parameter dimension grows. Moreover, it can incorporate differential-equation constraints, including in nonlinear settings. Overall, these results indicate that uncertainty-quantified neural surrogates can enable scalable and reliable Bayesian inference for inverse problems in complex systems.

</details>


### [192] [How Transparent is DiffusionGemma?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20560)
*Joshua Engels,Callum McDougall,Bilal Chughtai,Janos Kramar,Senthoran Rajamanoharan,Cindy Wu,Arthur Conmy,Asic Q Chen,Jean Tarbouriech,Min Ma,Brendan O'Donoghue,João Gabriel Lopes de Oliveira,Rohin Shah,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散语言模型DiffusionGemma的推理透明度问题。虽然表面上其不透明序列深度是自回归模型Gemma 4的28.6倍，但通过引入可解释的token瓶颈映射中间状态，可将该差距缩小至1.1倍。研究发现DiffusionGemma具有与Gemma 4相当的可监控性，并揭示了扩散模型特有的现象（如非时序推理、token/序列涂抹等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理透明度对于理解模型决策、缓解误用和错位、调试异常行为至关重要。扩散语言模型在连续潜在空间中执行大量计算，这引发了其推理透明度是否会降低的核心问题，亟需系统性研究。

Method: 将透明度分解为两个维度：变量透明度（理解模型计算状态中间快照的能力）和算法透明度（利用快照重构模型输出过程的能力）。通过构建可解释的token瓶颈来映射去噪步骤间的信息流，保持下游性能不下降，并开展可解释性案例研究。

Result: 通过将中间状态视为可解释状态，不透明序列深度从28.6倍降至1.1倍。发现了扩散模型特有的新型现象：非时序推理、token和序列涂抹、中间上下文推理。DiffusionGemma的可监控性与Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型虽然在算法透明度方面比自回归模型更具挑战性（因为所有token预测在每步去噪中都可改变），但其透明度可通过适当方法大幅提升。初步证据表明扩散模型存在独特的可解释性现象，为未来研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: LLM reasoning transparency is a critical affordance for understanding model decisions, mitigating misuse and misalignment, and debugging surprising model behaviors. However, DiffusionGemma performs a larger fraction of its computation in a continuous latent space; does this make its reasoning less transparent? We study this question by decomposing transparency into two components: variable transparency, whether we understand intermediate snapshots of a model's computational state; and algorithmic transparency, whether we can use these snapshots to reconstruct the process by which the model arrived at its outputs. Naively, DiffusionGemma has poor variable transparency: its opaque serial depth, the amount of serial computation that occurs in between interpretable model states, seems at first 28.6X higher than the corresponding autoregressive Gemma 4 model. However, we show that we can map the information flowing between denoising steps through an interpretable token bottleneck with no decrease in downstream performance. Treating these intermediate states as interpretable reduces the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X that of Gemma 4. Algorithmic transparency is harder for diffusion models than for autoregressive models because all token predictions in the canvas can change at every denoising step, giving the model the power to implement complicated distributed algorithms during the denoising process. To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.

</details>


### [193] [Sparsity, Superposition, and Forgetting: A Mechanistic Study of Representation Retention in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20431)
*Jan Wasilewski,Jędrzej Kozal,Michał Woźniak,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受控的玩具世界框架来研究持续学习中的遗忘机制，发现叠加并不必然导致遗忘，其效果受表示强度和容量分配的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习系统经常遗忘先前获取的知识，但在实际数据集中，驱动遗忘的机制难以分离，因为真实数据集纠缠了许多因素。为了解决这一困难，需要一个可控的框架使这些机制可观察和可测试。

Method: 使用合成生成器-分离器管道，定义真值潜在特征，构建具有可调稀疏性和重叠性的任务，并引入表示强度和叠加（特征间的方向重叠）的可测量量。通过拟合保留、叠加和暴露历史之间的稀疏动态关系（使用SINDy）来研究保留动力学。同时，基于有效秩的任务级分析表征了表示容量在任务间的分配方式。

Result: 实验得出三个主要发现：(1) 叠加倾向于随时间增加，但在任务边界处会出现短暂下降，表明是边界特定的干扰而非稳定漂移；(2) 更高的特征稀疏性引起更多叠加，但不必然导致遗忘；当表示保持强健时，即使存在重叠也能减少遗忘；(3) 任务级有效秩随稀疏性增长，表明在稀疏机制下使用了更广泛的容量。

Conclusion: 这些结果细化了"更多叠加导致更多遗忘"的常见直觉，表明重叠与表示强度和容量分配相互作用。该玩具分析为持续学习提供了可证伪的假设和诊断工具。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) systems often forget previously acquired knowledge, yet the mechanisms driving forgetting remain hard to isolate in practice because real datasets entangle many factors. We present a controlled, toy-world framework that makes these mechanisms observable and testable. Using a synthetic generator-separator pipeline, we define ground-truth latent features, build tasks with tunable sparsity and overlap, and introduce measurable quantities for representation strength and superposition (directional overlap among features). We then study retention dynamics-the temporal change of representation strength by fitting sparse dynamical relations (via SINDy) between retention, superposition, and exposure history. A complementary task-level analysis based on effective rank characterizes how representational capacity is allocated across tasks. Our controlled experiments yield three takeaways. (1) Superposition tends to increase over time with transient dips at task boundaries, suggesting boundary-specific interference rather than steady drift. (2) Higher feature sparsity induces more superposition yet does not inevitably cause forgetting; when representations remain strong, forgetting can be reduced despite overlap. (3) Task-level effective rank grows with sparsity, indicating broader capacity usage under sparse regimes. Together, these results nuance the common intuition that more superposition leads to more forgetting by showing that overlap interacts with representation strength and capacity allocation. Our toy analysis provides falsifiable hypotheses and diagnostic tools for CL.

</details>


### [194] [Evolutionary Two-Stage Hyperparameter Optimization Strategies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20442)
*Fedor Buzaev,Dmitry Efremenko,Egor Bugaev,Andrei Ermakov,Denis Derkach,Daria Pugacheva,Fedor Ratnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对PINNs训练中的超参数敏感性问题，提出了一种基于进化算法的两阶段优化方法，在有限计算资源下显著降低了求解误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在求解偏微分方程时存在收敛不稳定、训练平台期以及对架构和优化超参数高度敏感的问题。由于物理信息损失函数具有高度非凸和多结构特性，传统的局部或基于梯度的超参数搜索策略容易陷入次优区域。

Method: 提出了一种基于进化算法的两阶段方法。第一阶段使用截断训练周期的低保真度训练快速筛选候选配置，将超参数选择视为黑盒外循环优化问题；第二阶段仅对最有希望的候选配置使用标准梯度优化器进行完整训练以优化解。

Result: 在Advection、Klein-Gordon和Helmholtz三个典型偏微分方程问题上进行评估，该方法在受限计算资源下始终优于标准训练方法，实现了显著更低的平均误差。

Conclusion: 进化算法结合两阶段策略能够有效解决PINNs的超参数优化问题，在固定计算预算下提高了解的精度和鲁棒性，为PINNs的实际应用提供了更可靠的训练框架。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) solve Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws into neural network training. However, their performance suffers from unstable convergence, training plateaus, and strong sensitivity to architectural and optimization hyperparameters due to the highly non-convex and multi-term structure of the physics-informed loss. In this setting, the outer-loop hyperparameter search is a noisy and black-box optimization problem over heterogeneous parameters, where classical local or gradient-based strategies are easily trapped in suboptimal regions. Evolutionary algorithms, with their population-based exploration and ability to handle mixed, non-differentiable search spaces, provide a more robust mechanism for discovering promising configurations. We propose and investigate a two-stage approach based on evolutionary algorithms that combines exploration and exploitation parts of PINNs training to improve solution accuracy and robustness under fixed computational budgets. In the first stage, we perform low-fidelity training runs with truncated epochs to rapidly screen candidate configurations, treating hyperparameter selection as a black-box outer-loop problem. In the second stage, only the most promising candidates are fully trained with standard gradient-based optimizers to refine the solution. Evaluated on three popular problems, namely Advection, Klein-Gordon and Helmholtz equations, our method consistently outperforms standard training and achieves significantly lower mean error within constrained computational resources.

</details>


### [195] [Data Bias Mitigation under Coverage Constraints & The Price of Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20461)
*Bruno Scarone,Alfredo Viola,Renée J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了偏见缓解框架，通过引入覆盖约束来解决机器学习模型中交叉敏感属性（如种族和性别）的歧视性问题，在满足覆盖约束的前提下，用小的近似误差换取更高的数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型对具有多个敏感属性交叉（如种族和性别）的个体表现出歧视性结果或性能下降，这源于两个相互关联的挑战：缺乏量化（潜在交叉）偏见的原则性度量方法，以及训练数据中交叉子群体代表性不足。

Method: 扩展了一个偏见缓解框架，加入覆盖约束以确保各群体（包括交叉子群体）的充分代表性；将偏见缓解表述为整数线性规划，在所有缓解策略上进行优化，并刻画公平性代价，即公平性容忍度的函数，从而实现数据效率与偏见消除之间的权衡。

Result: 在公开数据集上的评估表明，通过该框架进行偏见缓解能在多个分类器上保持预测准确性；覆盖约束虽然在统计考虑下提出，但对于保持下游机器学习性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 该方法在满足法律合规性（法规可能要求特定的公平性阈值）和数据治理需求的同时，能够有效缓解交叉性偏见，为从业者在偏见降低与数据修改成本（尤其是数据购买成本）之间做出明智的权衡提供了工具。

Abstract: Machine learning models have been shown to exhibit discriminatory outcomes or degraded performance for individuals at the intersection of multiple sensitive attributes, such as race and gender. This stems in part from two interrelated challenges: the lack of principled measures for quantifying bias (potentially intersectional), and insufficient representation of intersectional subgroups in training data. We extend a recent bias mitigation framework to incorporate coverage constraints that enforce sufficient representation across groups, including intersectional subgroups. Since achieving exactly zero bias for all groups may not be data efficient (meaning it may require large amounts of data), our solution trades small approximation errors in bias for greater data efficiency while satisfying coverage constraints. We also formulate bias mitigation as an integer linear program that optimizes over all mitigation strategies, and characterize the price of fairness, the minimum data modification cost, as a function of fairness tolerance. This is essential both for legal compliance, where regulations may mandate specific fairness thresholds, and for data governance, enabling practitioners to make informed trade-offs between bias reduction and data modification (particularly, data purchasing) costs. We evaluate our techniques on publicly available datasets, demonstrating that bias mitigation via our framework preserves predictive accuracy across multiple classifiers, and that coverage constraints, while motivated by statistical considerations, are essential for preserving downstream ML performance.

</details>


### [196] [Agentic Symbolic Search: Characterizing PDEs Beyond Hand-crafted Expressions, Meshes, and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20467)
*Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ASYS的框架，利用先验知识和进化搜索自动发现PDE解的可解释符号形式，超越了传统数值和神经网络方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学家通过数学结构而非数值表来理解PDE解，传统上依赖手工分析，而数值模拟和神经网络无法直接生成这些结构。

Method: ASYS是一个先验引导的智能体框架，将PDE理论、约束和搜索经验转化为可测试的符号程序。采用进化搜索优化数学形式，结合梯度优化拟合连续参数，实现自动化的归纳偏置注入。

Result: 在五个涵盖有界动力学、有限时间爆破和自由边界聚焦的问题中，ASYS成功恢复已知解析形式，并为Allen-Cahn 2D动力学发现了几何界面公式，为Keller-Segel趋化爆破发现了九参数收缩律。

Conclusion: ASYS展示了表征PDE解的新范式，能够生成可解释的解析近似，为数学家的进一步分析提供指导，超越了手工分析、网格数值解和神经网络近似。

Abstract: Mathematicians understand a PDE solution through mathematical structures rather than tables of computed values. Historically, this has been the product of mathematical analysis, carried out by hand for each problem individually. Neither numerical simulation nor neural networks produce those structures directly. We propose Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework in which an agent translates PDE theory, public problem constraints, and accumulated search experience into testable differentiable symbolic programs. The mathematical forms are refined under evolutionary search, while their continuous parameters are fit by gradient-based optimization. This makes the search an automated form of inductive-bias injection rather than blind symbolic regression. For problems with known analytical forms, ASYS recovers these forms naturally; for other problems, ASYS constructs analytical approximations which can guide mathematicians toward further analysis. In our experiments, across five problems spanning bounded dynamics, finite-time blow-up, and free-boundary focusing, ASYS produces interpretable representations, including a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn 2D dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel chemotactic blow-up, in settings where no closed-form description was previously available. ASYS shows the possibility of a new paradigm for characterizing PDE solutions, beyond handcrafted analytical solutions, mesh-based numerical solutions, and neural network approximations.

</details>


### [197] [Fisher-Geometric Sharpness and the Implicit Bias of SGD toward Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20469)
*Md Sakir Ahmed,Kumaresh Sarmah,Hemen Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了黎曼锐度（SR）的概念，解决了传统平坦性度量在重参数化下不变性的问题，证明了SGD倾向于收敛到黎曼平坦的极小值，并建立了其与泛化性能的理论联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中广泛认为SGD隐式地偏好平坦极小值，且平坦极小值泛化性能更好。然而，传统的欧几里得平坦性度量（如损失Hessian矩阵的迹或最大特征值）在保持网络函数不变的重参数化下不具有不变性，这动摇了该理论的基础。为了解决这一问题，本文试图在Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）诱导的统计流形的黎曼几何基础上重新定义平坦性。

Method: 1. 基于FIM诱导的统计流形的黎曼几何，数学定义了黎曼锐度（Riemannian Sharpness）。
2. 证明黎曼锐度在光滑、保持函数不变的重参数化下具有不变性。
3. 将小批量SGD的梯度噪声形式化为具有与FIM成比例的协方差结构。
4. 推导了所得随机微分方程的平稳分布。
5. 利用PAC-Bayes泛化界将几何偏差与测试性能联系起来。

Result: 1. 证明了平稳分布的概率质量在黎曼平坦极小值处呈指数集中。
2. PAC-Bayes泛化界表明黎曼锐度显式控制泛化性能。
3. MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验证实，黎曼锐度能有效追踪泛化性能，而欧几里得锐度则不能。
4. 实验验证了黎曼锐度随$η/B$的缩放关系与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 本文为“平坦极小值泛化性能更好”提供了严格且重参数化不变的理论解释。通过将平坦性建立在黎曼几何基础上，解决了传统欧几里得度量缺乏不变性的问题，从理论和实验上证明了SGD通过梯度噪声结构自然地偏向黎曼平坦的解，从而实现更好的泛化。

Abstract: A widely held intuition in deep learning is that stochastic gradient descent (SGD) implicitly favors flat minima and that flat minima generalize better, but standard Euclidean measures of flatness such as the trace or maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian are not invariant under reparametrizations that preserve the network function, which undermines the theoretical foundations of this narrative. In this study we resolve this issue by grounding flatness in the Riemannian geometry of the statistical manifold induced by the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM). We define Riemannian sharpness mathematically and prove that it is invariant under smooth, function-preserving reparametrizations, which directly addresses the critique of Dinh et al. in the paper ``Sharp minima can generalize for deep nets''.We note that this invariance is a property of the true FIM; the diagonal empirical estimator used in practice (and in all experiments below) inherits invariance only approximately, and exact invariance under arbitrary reparametrizations would require structured estimators such as K-FAC. We formalize the gradient noise of mini-batch SGD as having a covariance structure proportional to the FIM, derive the stationary distribution of the resulting stochastic differential equation, and then show that the probability mass is exponentially concentrated at Riemannian-flat minima. A PAC-Bayes generalization bound controlled explicitly by SR formally links this geometric bias to test performance. Our experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 confirm that SR reliably tracks generalization in ways that Euclidean sharpness does not, and that its scaling with $η/B$ matches the theoretical predictions. Together these results provide a rigorous, reparametrization-invariant account of why flat minima generalize.

</details>


### [198] [Marginal Advantage Accumulation for Memory-Driven Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20475)
*Mingyu Yang,Keye Zheng,Congchao Cheng,Yujie Liu,Xingkang Lu,Fan Jiang,Yefei Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出边际优势累积(MAA)方法，通过跨批次的操作级证据累积机制解决批量式轨迹蒸馏中反馈矛盾问题，在4个基准上16个设置中14个取得最佳结果，同时降低约75%的优化阶段token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量式轨迹蒸馏中，相同的内存操作在不同批次间可能收到矛盾反馈，现有方法缺乏跨批次、操作级的证据累积机制，无法区分稳定有效操作与偶然命中，这是制约蒸馏性能的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出边际优势累积(MAA)后处理架构：(1)形式化为可对齐性和可比较性两个结构条件；(2)构建跨批次可比较的差分信号；(3)通过EMA累积每个操作的有符号证据；(4)通过语义身份合并确保跨批次可追溯性。

Result: 在4个基准和4个目标模型的16个设置中，MAA在14个设置上取得最佳结果，持续超越现有批量级蒸馏基线，在多数设置中匹敌或超越在线替代方案，并将优化阶段token消耗降低约75%。

Conclusion: MAA作为首个跨批次操作级证据累积机制，有效解决了批量式轨迹蒸馏中的反馈矛盾问题，在不引入在线计算开销的前提下实现了性能与效率的双重提升，为轨迹蒸馏提供了新的技术范式。

Abstract: In batch-style trace distillation, the same memory operation may receive contradictory feedback across different batches. Existing methods lack a cross-batch, operation-level evidence accumulation mechanism, making it impossible to distinguish stably effective operations from accidental hits. This paper formalizes the requirement as two structural conditions, alignability and comparability, and proposes Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA). MAA constructs differential signals to make them comparable across batches, accumulates signed evidence per operation via EMA, and ensures cross-batch traceability through semantic identity merging. As a post-processing architecture, MAA achieves the best results in 14 out of 16 settings across 4 benchmarks and 4 target models, consistently outperforming existing batch-level distillation baselines and matching or surpassing online alternatives in most settings, while reducing optimization-phase token consumption by approximately 75%.

</details>


### [199] [Multi-Task Bayesian In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20538)
*Qingyang Zhu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多任务上下文学习框架，用于分摊式分层贝叶斯预测推理，通过在上下文数据集中显式编码先验信息，实现了跨先验族的自适应预测，在保持贝叶斯预测器性能的同时实现了数量级的计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯预测推理提供了不确定性量化、数据效率和鲁棒泛化的理论框架，但精确推断通常难以处理。现有的可扩展近似方法计算成本高或需要限制性建模假设；Prior-Data Fitted和上下文模型虽提供了分摊式替代方案，但与训练先验的支持集紧密耦合，缺乏适应新先验的机制，在分布偏移下鲁棒性有限。

Method: 提出多任务上下文学习框架用于分摊式分层贝叶斯预测推理，将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀。在先验任务和目标任务序列上训练Transformer，使其学习跨先验族自适应预测。该方法能够在测试时适应新的先验分布，无需重新训练。

Result: 在难度递增的评估套件上（包括元分布外先验和具有高维潜在结构的先验），该方法与贝叶斯预测器的性能相匹配，同时实现数量级的计算加速。在真实世界的时空温度预测基准测试上验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功解决了现有分摊式贝叶斯推理方法在先验适应性和分布偏移鲁棒性方面的局限性，通过显式编码先验信息实现了跨先验族的自适应预测，为贝叶斯预测推理提供了一个高效、鲁棒且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Bayesian predictive inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, data efficiency, and robust generalization. However, exact inference is often intractable, and scalable approximations may remain computationally expensive or require restrictive modeling assumptions that degrade predictive performance. Prior-Data Fitted and in-context models have recently emerged as an amortized alternative by learning to map datasets directly to predictive distributions, but existing approaches are tightly coupled to the support of the training prior and lack explicit mechanisms for adapting to new priors at test time, resulting in limited robustness under distribution shift. We introduce a multi-task in-context learning framework for amortized hierarchical Bayesian predictive inference that explicitly represents prior information as a prefix of in-context datasets. A transformer trained on sequences of prior and target tasks learns to adapt its predictions across families of priors. On a suite of evaluations with increasing difficulty, including out-of-meta-distribution priors and priors with high-dimensional latent structures, our method matches oracle Bayesian predictors while being orders of magnitude faster. We further demonstrate its practical relevance on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/.

</details>


### [200] [Predictability as a Fine-Grained Measure for Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20546)
*Linda Lu,Karthik Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“可预测性隐私”框架，通过显式引入攻击者的核心知识（被泄露的数据部分）和特定查询族，提供比差分隐私更细粒度的隐私度量与ERM输出扰动方案，两者可互补使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私提供严格的个人隐私保证，但其最坏情况特性往往导致昂贵的隐私-精度权衡。本文旨在提出一种更细粒度的隐私框架，显式地将攻击者的核心知识与特定敏感信息纳入考量，以缓解该权衡。

Method: 提出通过可预测性定义隐私的框架，将隐私泄露量化为攻击者在观测输出后对未知个体敏感信息的预测能力增量。基于广义矩方法，建立对被泄露数据为平稳、遍历、混合过程时的渐近可预测性分析框架，并推导出针对经验风险最小化（ERM）的、按可预测性校准的输出扰动方案。

Result: 可预测性与差分隐私一般不可比，各自可在对方较大时仍保持较小；在除一人外全部被泄露的极限情形下可预测性蕴含互信息DP；所提ERM输出扰动方案可有效控制可预测性，且该方法与DP互补。

Conclusion: 可预测性提供了一种更细粒度的隐私度量，可针对特定敏感信息与攻击者模型定制；所提方法与差分隐私兼容并可协同使用，从而实现更精细的隐私控制。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) ensures rigorous individual-level privacy guarantees against even the most knowledgeable attackers, but its worst-case nature can impose a costly privacy-accuracy tradeoff. We introduce privacy via predictability, a fine-grained framework that explicitly incorporates the attacker's core knowledge, a compromised portion of the dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a specified family of queries. Predictability measures privacy leakage as the incremental gain in an attacker's ability to predict sensitive information about unknown individuals after observing the algorithm's output, beyond what can already be inferred from the compromised data. We show that predictability and DP are generally incomparable: each can be small while the other is large. However, in the worst-case regime where all but one individual is compromised, and all binary queries are considered sensitive, predictability implies mutual-information DP. More generally, predictability provides a finer-grained privacy metric tailored to specific sensitive information and specific attacker models. We introduce a general framework, using the generalized method of moments (GMM), to analyze asymptotic predictability when the compromised data is generated by a stationary, ergodic, mixing process. Using this analysis, we derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM. Our approach is complementary to DP and can be used alongside DP to provide fine-grained privacy control.

</details>


### [201] [The Token Is a Group Element: On Lie-Algebra Attention over Matrix Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20547)
*Przemyslaw Musialski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次提出Lie代数注意力机制，将矩阵李群元素作为token，使用相对姿态的代数范数作为闭式注意力分数，无需表示论工具或学习核函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于不可约表示或满射指数方法的注意力机制无法处理非紧致非阿贝尔仿射群（含缩放和剪切变换），且需要复杂的表示论工具（球谐函数、Clebsch-Gordan积等）。作者希望构建一种更简洁的群等变注意力机制，直接将李群元素作为token，避免传统表示论工具的复杂性。

Method: 将矩阵李群G的元素$g_i$作为attention token（无特征负载），通过群内禀的相对几何$g_i^{-1}g_j$定义不变量$w_{ij}=\log(g_i^{-1}g_j)$，使用负平方代数范数$s_{ij}=-\|\log(g_i^{-1}g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$作为闭式注意力分数（块加权Frobenius内积下的标准邻近核）。对角G作用下的等变性自动满足，cocycle条件自动成立。方法适用于任何矩阵李群的对数图，包括SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)。

Result: 在SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)上的序列补全实验表明：闭式分数与相同不变量上的MLP学习核性能相当，在SE(2)上优于学习核，同时参数量减少50-80倍；向量token基线方法破坏不变性达5-12个数量级。方法成功扩展到传统irrep和满射指数方法无法处理的非紧致非阿贝尔仿射群。

Conclusion: Lie代数注意力提供了一种简洁优雅的李群等变注意力构造，无需表示论工具或学习核函数，可处理更广泛的矩阵李群（包括非紧致非阿贝尔群），在保持或提升性能的同时大幅减少参数量，为群等变深度学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: We place the attention token on the group: a token is an element $g_i$ of a matrix Lie group $G$ -- a bare transformation, with no feature payload and no external action $ρ(g)$ carrying it. To our knowledge this is the first attention construction whose tokens are bare matrix Lie group elements: their score is the closed-form algebra norm of the relative pose rather than a learned kernel, and it reaches the affine full-frame groups that every irrep- or surjective-exp-based method must exclude. We call it Lie-Algebra Attention. Once tokens are group elements, the rest follows with none of the usual representation-theoretic machinery. The relative geometry of a pair is canonical, $g_i^{-1} g_j$, so the pairwise invariant $w_{ij} = \log(g_i^{-1} g_j)$ is intrinsic rather than designed; equivariance under the diagonal $G$-action is tautological, and the cocycle condition holds automatically. The attention score is the negative squared algebra norm, $s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1} g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$: the canonical proximity kernel under a block-weighted Frobenius inner product, with no irreducible representations, spherical harmonics, Clebsch-Gordan products, or learned kernel. The construction applies to any matrix Lie group on a chosen logarithm chart containing the relative poses, including the non-compact non-abelian affine groups with scale and shear that no vector-token attention method reaches: neither the irrep tradition nor surjective-exp methods. Three sequence-completion experiments, on SE(2), SO(3), and Aff(2), bear this out: the closed-form score matches a learned MLP kernel on the same invariant and outperforms it on SE(2), using 50 to 80x fewer score parameters, while a vector-token baseline breaks invariance by five to twelve orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [202] [Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20557)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个达到极小极大最优样本复杂度$\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-3})$的确定性多重校准算法，证明了随机化对于最优样本复杂度并非必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前所有达到最优样本复杂度的多重校准预测器都是随机化的，而确定性预测器的样本复杂度明显更差。[CLNR26]等先前工作明确提出了随机化对于多重校准最优样本复杂度是否必要这一开放性问题。

Method: 作者设计了一种新的算法，能够输出确定性预测器并达到极小极大最优样本复杂度，然后将该算法推广到产生满足结果不可区分性(OI)的最优确定性预测器。

Result: 成功构造出样本复杂度为$\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-3})$的确定性多重校准预测器，并推广至有限或有限覆盖测试集合的结果不可区分性，同时给出了具有最优样本复杂度的确定性全预测器和泛预测器。

Conclusion: 证明了随机化对于多重校准的最优样本复杂度并非必要，同时解决了[OKK25]和[BHHLZ25]提出的关于确定性全预测器和泛预测器的开放性问题。

Abstract: A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works.
  We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [A Topos-Theoretic Interpretation of Blockchain Systems: Sheaves of Consensus and the Logic of Decentralized Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19519)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出使用拓扑斯理论(Topos Theory)作为区块链系统建模的原生数学语言，以替代传统的有限状态机模型，从而更准确地刻画去中心化环境下的共识过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的区块链系统形式化模型（主要是有限状态机或有标迁移系统）将共识机制——区块链最核心和最具创新性的部分—— relegation 为复杂的实现细节，排除在形式化模型之外。然而共识过程并非辅助特性，而是计算现象的本质所在。为忠实地建模这一过程，需要一种新的数学语言。

Method: 采用拓扑斯理论（Topos Theory）——层的范畴理论——作为建模区块链系统的数学框架。拓扑斯理论天然适用于描述由局部一致性定义并通过构建全局真值而工作的系统。

Result: 论证了拓扑斯理论为区块链系统提供了原生数学语言，能够形式化地刻画去中心化环境中的局部一致性与全局真理构建这一核心计算现象。

Conclusion: 拓扑斯理论是区块链系统形式化建模的合适数学基础，能够将共识机制纳入核心模型而非作为实现细节，从而更准确地反映区块链计算的本质特征。

Abstract: The predominant formal models for blockchain systems, particularly smart contracts, have largely been drawn from the classical theory of computation, with the finite state machine (FSM) or labeled transition system serving as the primary conceptual tool. However, the FSM relegates the most difficult and novel aspect of a blockchain -- the achievement of consensus in a decentralized environment -- to a complex, often messy, implementation detail that lies outside the formal model itself. But the process of consensus is not an ancillary feature; it is the very essence of the computational phenomenon. To model it faithfully, a new mathematical language is required. The central thesis of this work is that topos theory, the theory of categories of sheaves, provides the native mathematical language for systems defined by local consistency and the construction of global truth.

</details>


### [204] [Certified Euclidean-Residue Minimal-Alignment Switch Decompositions for Three Edge-Disjoint Hamiltonian Cycles in Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19832)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在自然Cayley几何下提出了一种基于局部交换演算的方法，实现了非互质Eisenstein-Jacobi网络的三条边不相交哈密顿环分解，其中前两条环各使用最小的d-1次交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eisenstein-Jacobi (EJ) 网络是度数为6的商格互连网络。当生成元参数gcd(a,b)=1时，三个自然单位方向自然形成三条边不相交的哈密顿环；但当gcd(a,b)=d>1时，每个单位方向分裂为d个环，边不相交哈密顿环问题转化为环拼接问题。现有方法使用矩形表示和交换调度证明存在性，但缺乏紧凑的构造性方法。

Method: 本文在自然Cayley几何下发展了一种不同的局部交换演算方法。前两条哈密顿环各使用最小可能的d-1次组件间交换来构建，第三条环作为未使用边补集获得。证明分为四个组成部分：组件标签坍缩、锚点取消、提升交换表示的非碰撞性，以及连通补关联。所有无限族定理均通过代数补关联证书符号证明，不依赖有限见证或计算枚举。

Result: 为欧几里得残差族提供了紧凑、公式驱动、最小交换的分解方法，并符号化证明了补关联性。该方法本质上是构造性的，给出了明确的交换次数下界（d-1）。虽然不覆盖所有非互质EJ网络，但在可写出代数补关联证书的参数范围内提供了完整解答。

Conclusion: 本文贡献不是为所有非互质EJ网络提出新的存在性定理，而是为特定参数范围提供了紧凑的公式化最小交换分解。表格和CSV数据仅用于验证和复现公式，不作为无限族定理的证明依据。该方法强调了代数证明相对于计算枚举的优越性。

Abstract: Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six quotient-lattice interconnection networks. For a generator $α=a+bρ$, let $N=a^2+ab+b^2$ and $d=\gcd(a,b)$. If $d=1$, the three natural unit directions already give three edge-disjoint Hamiltonian cycles. If $d>1$, each unit direction splits into $d$ cycles and the EDHC problem becomes a cycle-splicing problem. Existing non-coprime EJ decompositions prove existence by using a rectangular representation and exchange schedules. This paper develops a different, local switch calculus in the natural Cayley geometry. The first two Hamiltonian cycles are built using the minimum possible $d-1$ intercomponent switches each, and the third factor is obtained as the unused edge complement. The contribution is deliberately not a new existence theorem for all non-coprime EJ networks; rather, it is a compact, formula-driven, minimal-switch decomposition for Euclidean-residue families whose complement incidence is proved symbolically. The proof separates four ingredients: component-label collapse, anchor cancellation, noncollision of lifted switch representatives, and connected complement incidence. No infinite-family theorem in this manuscript is proved by finite witnesses or by computational enumeration. The theorem scope is stated for the parameter ranges where an algebraic complement-incidence certificate is written down. Tables and CSV data are used only to verify and reproduce the formulas, never as proof of an infinite-family theorem.

</details>


### [205] [The Sheaf Laplacian: A Topological Framework for Data Fusion and Consensus in Distributed Sensing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19529)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文主张传统基于简单图的经典网络模型不足以刻画现代分布式系统的复杂性，并指出引入层的理论提供了更富表达力的数学语言，且通过层拉普拉斯算子可支持分布式传感网络中的数据融合与共识建立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式系统由异构代理、高维多模态数据流以及复杂且依赖上下文的关系构成，传统简单图模型仅能通过简单连边或标量权重描述连接，难以充分表达这些复杂特征，因而需要更具表达能力的数学框架。

Method: 引入层理论作为描述复杂网络结构的高表达力数学语言，构建并应用层拉普拉斯算子作为分布式传感网络中数据融合与共识建立的机制。

Result: 证明了层拉普拉斯算子能够有效地在分布式传感网络中实现数据融合与共识建立，验证了层理论作为网络建模新语言的有效性。

Conclusion: 层理论提供了超越传统简单图模型的数学语言，能够更准确地刻画现代分布式系统的复杂性，层拉普拉斯算子为分布式网络中的关键任务（如数据融合、共识）提供了强有力的工具。

Abstract: We argue here that traditional network models, which are overwhelmingly based on the mathematical construct of a simple graph, are fundamentally insufficient for capturing the complexity of modern distributed systems. Such systems are characterized by heterogeneous agents with diverse capabilities, high-dimensional and multi-modal data streams, and intricate, context-dependent relationships that cannot be adequately described by a simple connection or a scalar weight. The limitations of these classical models necessitate a new mathematical language, one with far greater expressive power. We have found that sheaf theory provides us with such a language. Moreover, we show that the sheaf Laplacian is a suitable mechanism for data fusion and establishing consensus within distributed sensing networks.

</details>


### [206] [Fault-Tolerant Shared-Relay Communication in Circulant Interconnection Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19833)
*Bader Albader,Galal Hassan,Mohamed R. Al-Mulla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对有向循环网络中的两跳容错原语，建立了网络设计框架并发现生成器的选择对最坏情况下的中继生存性起决定性作用：优化阈值设计可在接近计数下界的程度内实现容错，而标准区间生成器即使度更大也可能在结构上失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环互连网络具有对称寻址、紧凑生成器描述和均匀本地连通性等优点，但需要研究在给定节点数n和度预算m的情况下，容错两跳原语的最坏情况共享中继复用度R(n,m)可以达到多大，以指导网络设计。

Method: 提出了循环差分-复用条件作为数学工具，围绕该工具构建网络设计框架，包括参数R(n,m)和D_f(n)、区间循环网络的否定定理、中继表预处理和查找算法、对抗性和随机故障保证、负载均衡范围、启发式设计的认证上界解释、精确小n校准、软件查找与搜索微基准测试，以及对526,539个生成器集的可重复研究。

Result: 生成器的选择对最坏情况下的中继生存性起决定性作用：优化阈值设计可在约1.16-1.63倍计数下界范围内实现f中继容错，而标准区间生成器即使在更大度的情况下也可能在结构上失败。

Conclusion: 该研究为有向循环网络的两跳容错设计提供了系统的理论框架和实用工具，证明了生成器优化对网络容错性能的关键影响，为实际网络设计提供了理论指导和定量基准。

Abstract: Circulant interconnection networks provide symmetric addressing, compact generator descriptions, and uniform local connectivity. This paper maps a degree--redundancy landscape for a fault-tolerant two-hop primitive in directed circulants: given $n$ nodes and degree budget $m$, how large can the worst-case shared-relay multiplicity $R(n,m)$ be? A node is a shared relay for an ordered terminal pair if it has outgoing links to both terminals; an $f$-relay-fault-tolerant circulant requires at least $f+1$ such relays for every pair. The underlying feasibility condition is a cyclic difference-multiplicity condition, which we use as a mathematical tool rather than claim as a new object. The contribution is the network-design framework around this tool: the parameters $R(n,m)$ and $D_f(n)$, a negative theorem for interval circulants, relay-table preprocessing and lookup algorithms, adversarial and random failure guarantees, load-balance scope, certified upper-bound interpretation of heuristic designs, exact small-$n$ calibration, a software lookup-versus-search microbenchmark, and a reproducible study of 526,539 generator sets. The results show that generator choice critically determines worst-case relay survivability: optimized threshold designs achieve $f$-relay-fault tolerance within about $1.16$--$1.63$ of the counting lower bound, while standard interval generators can fail structurally even at much larger degrees.

</details>


### [207] [SAC: Disaggregated KV Cache System for Sparse Attention LLMs with CXL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19746)
*Ruiyang Ma,Teng Ma,Junru Li,Hantian Zha,Xuchun Shang,Qingda Hu,Zheng Liu,Xinjun Yang,Tao Ma,Guojie Luo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SAC，首个针对稀疏注意力模型优化的分离式KV缓存系统。通过利用CXL的低延迟、缓存行粒度加载/存储语义，SAC仅按需获取所需的top-k KV条目，解决了传统RDMA方案在稀疏注意力模型中全量传输导致的瓶颈问题，实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM向长上下文推理扩展，内存容量成为主要服务瓶颈。传统基于RDMA的分离式内存池方案采用粗粒度方式获取整个前缀KV缓存，这对于新兴的稀疏注意力模型效率低下：虽然解码时只有小部分KV条目处于活跃状态，但这些系统仍然获取完整的KV缓存，导致严重的传输瓶颈和本地内存浪费。

Method: 提出SAC系统，这是首个为稀疏注意力模型优化的高效分离式KV缓存系统。通过利用Compute Express Link (CXL)的低延迟、缓存行粒度加载/存储语义，SAC在推理过程中仅按需获取所需的top-k KV条目，而非全量传输。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V3.2上使用SGLang进行的评估表明，与基于RDMA的基线相比，SAC实现了2.1倍更高的吞吐量、9.7倍更低的TTFT（Time To First Token）和1.8倍更低的TBT（Time Between Tokens）。

Conclusion: CXL分离架构被证明是新兴稀疏注意力模型的优越基础设施，能够有效解决传统RDMA方案在稀疏注意力场景下的效率问题，为长上下文LLM推理提供了更优的系统解决方案。

Abstract: The scaling of LLMs toward long-context inference has shifted the primary serving system bottleneck from computation to memory capacity. Traditional solutions for dense attention models rely on RDMA-based disaggregated memory pools, which perform coarse-grained fetching of the entire prefix KV cache from remote storage to local memory before decoding. However, this approach is fundamentally inefficient for emerging sparse attention models. While only a small fraction of KV entries are active during decoding, these systems still fetch the full KV cache locally, leading to severe transmission bottlenecks and local memory wastage. To address this, we propose SAC, the first efficient disaggregated KV cache system optimized for sparse attention models. By leveraging the low-latency, cache-line granularity load/store semantics of Compute Express Link (CXL), SAC fetches only the required top-k KV entries on demand during inference. Evaluations on DeepSeek-V3.2 using SGLang show that SAC achieves 2.1x higher throughput, 9.7x lower TTFT, and 1.8x lower TBT compared to RDMA-based baselines, establishing CXL-based disaggregation as the superior infrastructure for emerging sparse attention models.

</details>


### [208] [Multi-Orientation Edge-Minimum Repair for Non-Redundant Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19834)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了EJ-MOEM方法，用于解决密集Eisenstein-Jacobi网络中的非冗余一对全广播修复问题，证明了c-1条外部修复边的充分必要性以及深度不超过t+2的深度证书定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集Eisenstein-Jacobi网络是度数为6的代数互联网络，在实际应用中节点故障不可避免。现有研究需要解决如何在故障存在的情况下实现高效的非冗余一对全广播修复，即保证每个健康节点恰好接收一次消息、不使用故障节点、并保持原有健康树组件。

Method: 提出EJ-MOEM（多方向边最小修复）方法：评估固定规模的六边形广播树方向族，选择故障感知的候选树，将故障修剪树收缩为健康组件，使用外部组件交叉修复边重新连接这些组件。采用三带表示法、扇区后缀附加引理、非相邻扇区分离引理和六方向屏蔽分类法进行理论分析。

Result: 证明了：对于所选方向的故障修剪组件图连通的情况，恰好c-1条外部修复边是必要且充分的（c为健康组件数）；每个单故障放置存在深度不超过t+1的修复，每个双故障放置存在深度不超过t+2的修复。通过t=2到t=200的穷举测试、结构化测试和大规模随机测试，成功率达100%。

Conclusion: EJ-MOEM方法成功实现了密集EJ网络中的非冗余一对全广播修复，理论证明和大规模实验验证了方法的正确性和有效性，为代数互联网络的容错通信提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six algebraic interconnection networks whose finite quotient geometry is naturally represented by a hexagonal axial-coordinate ball. This paper studies non-redundant one-to-all broadcast repair in the dense EJ network generated by $α=(t+1)+tω$, where $t$ is the network diameter. We propose EJ-MOEM, a multi-orientation edge-minimum repair method that evaluates a constant-size family of hexagonal broadcast-tree orientations, selects a fault-aware candidate, contracts the fault-pruned tree into healthy components, and reconnects these components using external component-crossing repair edges. The resulting structure is a rooted spanning tree of the healthy subgraph: every healthy node receives the message exactly once, no faulty node is used, and the original healthy tree components are preserved. We prove that, for a chosen orientation whose fault-pruned component graph is connected, exactly $c-1$ external repair edges are necessary and sufficient, where $c$ is the number of healthy components. We also prove a depth-certificate theorem for EJ coordinate-reduction trees: every one-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+1$, and every two-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+2$. The proof uses the three-strip representation of EJ hexagons, a sector-suffix attachment lemma, a non-adjacent-sector separation lemma, and a six-direction shielding classification for paired cuts. Extended validation includes exhaustive one- and two-fault enumeration for $t=2,\ldots,12,14,16,18$ (up to $N=1027$ and 525,825 two-fault placements at $t=18$), structured theorem-critical tests through $t=30$, and large random tests through $t=200$, all with 100\% success and no violation of the theorem.

</details>


### [209] [EVM Workloads in the Wild: Evidence for Multi-Dimensional Gas Metering, State Growth, Delayed Execution, and Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19869)
*Lioba Heimbach,Kushal Babel,Jason Milionis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究通过实证分析发现EVM兼容区块链上的Gas计量假设（即执行条件稳定、资源比例固定）并不成立。Base和Ethereum在2025年的数据显示资源组合高度动态，且状态敏感性导致近半数Base交易的Gas估算在不同历史状态下出现变化，这对交易可预测性和多维Gas定价机制具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EVM兼容区块链的Gas计量机制假设执行条件是稳定的：资源组合足够恒定以支撑将执行成本压缩为具有固定相对价格的单个标量，且提交到执行之间的状态漂移不会实质性改变交易结果。本研究旨在测量该假设在多大程度上失效，为多维Gas计量和状态增长定价提供实证基础。

Method: 研究者在2025年全年对Ethereum (L1)和Base (L2)进行区块级测量研究，每日每条链采样3,000个区块。将每笔交易分解为操作码级执行Gas、内在Gas、退款和持久状态增量。为测量状态敏感性，研究者在较旧的状态上重新执行2025年9月的交易，记录Gas使用和存储访问模式的变化。

Result: 研究发现资源组合远非稳定：Base上存储读取和计算分别占执行Gas的29.2%和24.3%，而Ethereum将34.9%用于存储写入。Ethereum在2025年的Gas上限翻倍使其自身特征向计算更重的类Base模式转移。Base表现出更高比例的冷存储读取（49.7% vs Ethereum的39.6%）。持久状态增长在Base上达456GB，而Ethereum仅38GB。执行结果同样不稳定：46.0%的Base交易在附近历史状态下的Gas估算存在变化，而Ethereum为13.9%，MEV和DeFi活动敏感性尤其高。存储访问模式在状态间也存在分歧，限制了访问列表的有效性并使并行执行复杂化。

Conclusion: 本研究为多维Gas计量和状态增长的显式定价提供了实证基础。研究表明状态敏感的执行行为使工作负载估计复杂化，直接影响交易可预测性和用户体验，挑战了现有Gas计量机制的基本假设。

Abstract: Gas metering on EVM-compatible blockchains assumes that execution conditions are stable: that the resource mix is constant enough to justify collapsing execution costs into a single scalar with fixed relative prices, and that state drift between submission and execution does not materially alter a transaction's outcome. We measure the extent to which this assumption fails.
  We present a trace-level measurement study of EVM workloads on Ethereum (L1) and Base (L2) throughout 2025, sampling 3,000 blocks per day per chain. We decompose each transaction into opcode-level execution gas, intrinsic gas, refunds, and persistent state deltas. To measure state sensitivity, we re-execute transactions from September 2025 on older states and record how gas usage and storage access patterns change.
  We find the resource mix to be far from stable: on Base, storage reads and compute account for 29.2% and 24.3% of execution gas, while Ethereum devotes 34.9% to storage writes. Ethereum's gas limit doubling during 2025 shifted its own profile toward compute-heavier, Base-like patterns. Base also exhibits a higher fraction of cold storage reads (49.7% versus 39.6% on Ethereum). Persistent state growth, a permanent cost priced as a transient one, reaches 456 GB on Base versus 38 GB on Ethereum.
  Execution outcomes are equally unstable: gas estimates vary across nearby historical states for 46.0% of transactions on Base, compared to 13.9% on Ethereum, with especially high sensitivity for MEV and DeFi activity. Storage access patterns also diverge across states, limiting the effectiveness of access lists and complicating parallel execution.
  Our work provides an empirical foundation for multi-dimensional gas metering and explicit pricing of state growth. They show that state-sensitive execution behavior complicates workload estimation, directly affecting transaction predictability and user experience.

</details>


### [210] [Online Dynamic Batching with Formal Guarantees for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19989)
*Dian Li,Zekun Wang,Yaoru Wang,Jiahong Yan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出在线动态批处理（ODB）系统，解决现代LLM训练中样本真实成本在预处理后才可观测导致的批构建盲目性问题，在保持分布式训练同步的前提下实现1.58-4.43倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM训练打破了离线批采样器的核心假设：样本的真实训练成本（影响填充、内存使用和GPU饱和度）仅在预处理、数据增强、模板化、分词和多模态视觉token扩展后才可观测。除非维护依赖预处理和增强的长度缓存，否则批构建无法获知决定训练效率的关键信息。

Method: 提出在线动态批处理（ODB）系统，是一个DataLoader端的即插即用解决方案。将批形成移至准确可观测点，同时保持DDP步骤对齐。形式化分布式组对齐问题（DGAP），证明在默认连接模式身份覆盖和可选非连接样本配额闭包下具有无死锁有界终止性。无需修改模型、优化器或注意力kernel。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL 2B/8B模型上测试：相比固定批处理标准方法，单节点Full FT/LoRA吞吐量提升1.58-2.51倍，双节点Full FT提升1.71-3.78倍，生产MM-Mix达4.43倍。相比GMT/BMT离线token预算oracle方法，在UltraChat/LLaVA上差距在15%以内，在高CV的ShareGPT4o上更快：单节点Full FT/LoRA提升2.24-2.39倍，双节点Full FT提升3.06-3.69倍。质量与标准方法相当。

Conclusion: ODB成功填补了高异构性LLM微调的在线/即插即用领域：在质量相当的情况下实现大幅吞吐量提升，提供形式化DGAP保证，且无需长度缓存预计算或kernel重写。

Abstract: Modern LLM training breaks a core assumption behind offline batch samplers: the true training cost of a sample is only observable after preprocessing, augmentation, templating, tokenization, and multimodal visual-token expansion. Unless one pays for a preprocessing- and augmentation-dependent length cache, batch construction is therefore blind to the quantity that determines padding, memory use, and GPU saturation. We introduce Online Dynamic Batching (ODB), a DataLoader-side drop-in system that moves batch formation to this point of accurate observability while preserving DDP step alignment. We formalize this synchronization requirement as the Distributed Group Alignment Problem and prove deadlock-free bounded termination with default join-mode identity coverage and opt-in non-join sample-quota closure. ODB requires no model, optimizer, or attention-kernel changes and is released as online-dynamic-batching with lightweight trainer adapters. Across public 2B/8B Qwen3-VL runs on UltraChat/LLaVA/ShareGPT4o, ODB improves literal emitted-sample throughput vs. fixed-batch Standard by 1.58-2.51x on single-node Full FT/LoRA and 1.71-3.78x on two-node Full FT, with Standard-comparable quality; production MM-Mix reaches 4.43x. Against GMT/BMT offline token-budget oracles, ODB is within 15% on UltraChat/LLaVA and faster on high-CV ShareGPT4o: 2.24-2.39x single-node Full FT/LoRA and 3.06-3.69x two-node Full FT. Together, ODB occupies the online/drop-in regime for high-heterogeneity LLM fine-tuning: large throughput gains at Standard-comparable quality, formal DGAP guarantees, and no length-cache precompute or kernel rewrites.

</details>


### [211] [ARGUS: Production-Scale Tracing and Performance Diagnosis for over 10,000-GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20374)
*Jiasheng Zhou,Longbin Zeng,Clavis Chen,Ruiming Lu,Qinwei Yang,Leyi Ye,Ray Ying,Key Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ARGUS是一个面向万卡级GPU集群的低开销、细粒度、常驻式训练追踪与实时分析系统，在低于2%开销下实现约3700倍数据压缩，有效诊断计算掉队、链路降级等多种性能异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM训练需要常驻式、细粒度的可观测性以进行有效的性能诊断。粗粒度资源监控无法定位根因，而细粒度分析器产生5%-30%的过高开销和海量追踪数据，无法在生产集群中长期部署。

Method: ARGUS沿训练调用层级将观测分解为CPU调用栈、框架语义和GPU内核执行，实现低于2%的常驻开销；构建统一数据流水线，将原始内核事件压缩约3700倍；采用渐进式诊断框架，通过迭代级、阶段级和内核级分析自动隔离异常窗口、掉节点和降级内核。

Result: 在万卡GPU生产集群部署超过6个月，数据压缩率约3700倍（从每rank每步10MB压缩至2.7KB），支持持续慢速故障检测与性能优化；案例研究验证了对计算掉队、链路降级、流水线气泡放大、FlashAttention JIT停顿及被通信症状掩盖的计算掉队等多种典型异常的有效诊断。

Conclusion: ARGUS证明了在大规模生产集群中实现低开销、细粒度、常驻式追踪与实时分析的可行性，能够有效支持LLM训练的持续性能诊断与优化，为大规模训练系统的可观测性提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale LLM training requires always-on, fine-grained observability for effective performance diagnosis at scale. Coarse resource monitors alone cannot localize root causes, and fine-grained profilers incur prohibitive (5%-30%) overheads and massive trace volumes, making always-on deployment impractical in large production clusters.
  We propose ARGUS, a low-overhead, fine-grained, always-on tracing and real-time analysis system for training workloads in 10,000+ GPU-scale production clusters. ARGUS decomposes observation along the training call hierarchy into CPU call stacks, framework semantics, and GPU kernel execution, with always-on collection under a combined overhead of less than 2%. It builds a unified data pipeline and compresses raw kernel events by approximately 3,700x from 10 MB to 2.7 KB per rank per step. Its progressive diagnosis framework automatically isolates anomalous windows, straggler ranks, and degraded kernels through iteration-time, phase-level, and kernel-level analysis. Deployed for over six months on a 10,000+ GPU production cluster, ARGUS has supported continuous fail-slow detection and performance optimization. Our case studies further demonstrate its effectiveness across representative anomalies, including compute stragglers, link degradation, pipeline-bubble amplification, FlashAttention JIT stalls, and compute stragglers masked by communication symptoms.

</details>
